function AttackMultipleTargets(L, ...)

		return;
	end;
	pcall(function(...)

			return;
		end;
		local P = {};
		for L, j in pairs(L) do
			local V = j:FindFirstChild(EK(237692 + -190476));
			if V then
				table[EK(-826119 - (-873776))](P, { j, V });
			end;
		end;
		if #P == 685689050 % 4033465 then
			return;
		end;
		RegisterAttack:FireServer(-723238 - (-723238));
		RegisterHit:FireServer(P[529609 + -529608][39952 + -39950], P);
	end);
end;

		end);
	end;

end;

function getClosestPlayer(L, ...)
	local P = player[EK(1899198953 % 16232062)] and player[EK(1821906467 % 12651797)]:FindFirstChild(EK(-51439 - (-98960)));
	if not P then
		return nil;
	end;
	local j = L or maxRange;
	if AimlockTargetPlayer ~= EK(575473971 % 4081040) and AimlockTargetPlayer ~= nil then
		local L = Players:FindFirstChild(AimlockTargetPlayer);
		if L and (L[EK(729422 - 681723)] and L[EK(-429555 + 477254)]:FindFirstChild(EK(-643193 + 690714))) then
			local P = L[EK(290897 + -243198)]:FindFirstChildOfClass(EK(967387 - 920202));
			if P and P[EK(565714 + -518569)] > -731452 + 731452 then
				return L[EK(419260419 % 10480318)];
			end;
		end;
	end;

function ClearAura(...)
	for L, P in pairs(AuraObjects) do
		if P and P[EK(3886438666 % 15927834)] then
			P:Destroy();
		end;
	end;
	AuraObjects = {};
end;
function ApplyAura(L, ...)
	ClearAura();
	if not L then
		ActiveAura = nil;
		return;
	end;
	local P = AURA_DEFS[L];
	if not P then
		ActiveAura = nil;
		return;
	end;
	ActiveAura = L;
	local j = player[EK(671191 - 623492)];
	if not j then
		return;
	end;
	local V = j:FindFirstChild(EK(1746478765 % 14198628));
	if not V then
		return;
	end;
	pcall(function(...)
		local L = Instance[EK(-163766 + 210953)](EK(-692927 + 740444));
		L[EK(-414193 - (-462020))] = EK(891631 + -843956);

		L[EK(2543845526 % 12171284)] = V;
